+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+
 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

+            chain_residual == 0,

+            f"is {chain_residual}",
+        )

+def finite_covariance_proxy_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Stress the proxy cross-entropy asymptotic on anisotropic atomic laws."""
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    sample_power = 18
+    times = (4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+
+    for d in (4, 7):
+        sobol = qmc.Sobol(d=d + 1, scramble=True, seed=1729 + d)
+        uniform = sobol.random_base2(sample_power)
+        normal = ndtri(np.clip(uniform, 1.0e-14, 1.0 - 1.0e-14))
+        y = normal[:, :d] / np.abs(normal[:, d, None])
+        y_norm_sq = np.sum(y * y, axis=1)
+
+        pair_masses = np.linspace(0.45 / d, 0.75 / d, d)
+        pair_masses *= 0.72 / np.sum(pair_masses)
+        radii = np.linspace(0.65, 2.15, d)
+        covariance = np.diag(pair_masses * radii**2)
+        trace = float(np.trace(covariance))
+        covariance_zero = covariance - trace * np.eye(d) / d
+        iso = 3 * (d + 1) * (7 * d + 9) / (
+            4 * d * (d + 3) * (d + 5) * (d + 7)
+        )
+        traceless = 3 * (d + 1) * (d + 3) / (4 * (d + 5) * (d + 7))
+        q_value = iso * trace**2 + traceless * float(
+            np.sum(covariance_zero * covariance_zero)
+        )
+
+        quadratic = np.einsum("ni,ij,nj->n", y, covariance, y)
+        b_mode = (d + 1) / 2 * (
+            -trace / (1 + y_norm_sq)
+            + (d + 3) * quadratic / (1 + y_norm_sq) ** 2
+        )
+
+        cross_ratios = []
+        scaled_defects = []
+        for time in times:
+            quotient = np.full(y.shape[0], 1.0 - np.sum(pair_masses))
+            for axis, (mass, radius) in enumerate(zip(pair_masses, radii)):
+                shift = radius / time
+                dot = y[:, axis]
+                plus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 - 2 * shift * dot
+                minus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 + 2 * shift * dot
+                quotient += 0.5 * mass * (
+                    ((1 + y_norm_sq) / plus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                    + ((1 + y_norm_sq) / minus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                )
+
+            delta = quotient - 1
+            u = b_mode / time**2
+            remainder = delta - u
+            proxy_entropy = np.mean(
+                (1 + u) * np.log1p(u) - u
+                + remainder * np.log1p(u)
+            )
+            proxy_divergence = np.mean(
+                (1 + delta) * np.log((1 + delta) / (1 + u))
+                - remainder
+            )
+            cross_ratios.append(time**4 * proxy_entropy / q_value)
+            scaled_defects.append(time**4 * proxy_divergence)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            all(np.isfinite(cross_ratios))
+            and all(np.isfinite(scaled_defects))
+            and all(value >= -2.0e-10 for value in scaled_defects)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < (0.03 if quick else 0.012)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < abs(cross_ratios[0] - 1.0)
+            and scaled_defects[-1] < scaled_defects[0]
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}: t^4 cross/Q={cross_ratios}, "
+            f"t^4 D(h||k)={scaled_defects}"
+        )
+
+    return Check(
+        "finite-covariance proxy asymptotic stress",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+

     """Stress the uniform Phi-Bregman bound at zero density and large spikes."""

     return Check("critical Bregman perturbation bound", passed, "; ".join(rows))
 
 
+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+
 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

+def finite_covariance_proxy_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Stress the proxy cross-entropy asymptotic on anisotropic atomic laws."""
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    sample_power = 18
+    times = (4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+
+    for d in (4, 7):
+        sobol = qmc.Sobol(d=d + 1, scramble=True, seed=1729 + d)
+        uniform = sobol.random_base2(sample_power)
+        normal = ndtri(np.clip(uniform, 1.0e-14, 1.0 - 1.0e-14))
+        y = normal[:, :d] / np.abs(normal[:, d, None])
+        y_norm_sq = np.sum(y * y, axis=1)
+
+        pair_masses = np.linspace(0.45 / d, 0.75 / d, d)
+        pair_masses *= 0.72 / np.sum(pair_masses)
+        radii = np.linspace(0.65, 2.15, d)
+        covariance = np.diag(pair_masses * radii**2)
+        trace = float(np.trace(covariance))
+        covariance_zero = covariance - trace * np.eye(d) / d
+        iso = 3 * (d + 1) * (7 * d + 9) / (
+            4 * d * (d + 3) * (d + 5) * (d + 7)
+        )
+        traceless = 3 * (d + 1) * (d + 3) / (4 * (d + 5) * (d + 7))
+        q_value = iso * trace**2 + traceless * float(
+            np.sum(covariance_zero * covariance_zero)
+        )
+
+        quadratic = np.einsum("ni,ij,nj->n", y, covariance, y)
+        b_mode = (d + 1) / 2 * (
+            -trace / (1 + y_norm_sq)
+            + (d + 3) * quadratic / (1 + y_norm_sq) ** 2
+        )
+
+        cross_ratios = []
+        scaled_defects = []
+        for time in times:
+            quotient = np.full(y.shape[0], 1.0 - np.sum(pair_masses))
+            for axis, (mass, radius) in enumerate(zip(pair_masses, radii)):
+                shift = radius / time
+                dot = y[:, axis]
+                plus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 - 2 * shift * dot
+                minus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 + 2 * shift * dot
+                quotient += 0.5 * mass * (
+                    ((1 + y_norm_sq) / plus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                    + ((1 + y_norm_sq) / minus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                )
+
+            delta = quotient - 1
+            u = b_mode / time**2
+            remainder = delta - u
+            proxy_entropy = np.mean(
+                (1 + u) * np.log1p(u) - u
+                + remainder * np.log1p(u)
+            )
+            proxy_divergence = np.mean(
+                (1 + delta) * np.log((1 + delta) / (1 + u))
+                - remainder
+            )
+            cross_ratios.append(time**4 * proxy_entropy / q_value)
+            scaled_defects.append(time**4 * proxy_divergence)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            all(np.isfinite(cross_ratios))
+            and all(np.isfinite(scaled_defects))
+            and all(value >= -2.0e-10 for value in scaled_defects)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < (0.03 if quick else 0.012)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < abs(cross_ratios[0] - 1.0)
+            and scaled_defects[-1] < scaled_defects[0]
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}: t^4 cross/Q={cross_ratios}, "
+            f"t^4 D(h||k)={scaled_defects}"
+        )
+
+    return Check(
+        "finite-covariance proxy asymptotic stress",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+

+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+
 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

+\]

+ t^4H_d(t)\longrightarrow\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)

+ \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)\longrightarrow0.
+\]
+In particular, for the two laws \(\nu_{\rm ac}\) and \(\nu_{\rm cl}\) in
+Theorem~\ref{thm:critical-vague-tail-nonidentifiability},

  \mathcal C_{\nu_{\rm ac},\Sigma}(t)\longrightarrow0,
  \qquad

 \]
-Thus vague regular variation, including its spectral measure and slowly

+Thus the spectral measure and slowly varying factor do not determine the

 \end{corollary}
 
 \begin{proof}

 succeeded in 298ms:
    1: % sec_covariance_proxy_defect.tex -- exact covariance-proxy KL defect
    2: \section{The Covariance-Matched Poisson Defect}
    3: \label{sec:covariance-proxy-defect}
    4: 
    5: Finite covariance determines the quadratic mode but, in dimensions at least
    6: four, does not force the matched entropy to attain the corresponding
    7: coefficient.  The following result identifies the complete nonnegative
    8: remainder without imposing a tail class or an absolute moment above order
    9: two.
   10: 
   11: \begin{theorem}[Covariance-proxy defect decomposition]
   12: \label{thm:covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition}
   13: \label{thm:finite-covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition}
   14: Let \(d\ge1\), and let \(\nu\) be a probability law on \(\RR^d\) with mean
   15: \(\bar\gamma\) and finite covariance matrix \(\Sigma\).  Put
   16: \[
   17:  h_t=P_t^{(d)}*\nu,
   18:  \qquad g_t=P_t^{(d)}(\mathord\cdot-\bar\gamma),
   19:  \qquad H_d(t)=D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|g_t),
   20: \]
   21: and let \(b_\Sigma\) be the bounded covariance mode in
   22: \eqref{eq:multidim-leading-theorem-density-mode}.  For all sufficiently
   23: large \(t\),
   24: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-poisson-proxy}
   25:  k_{\Sigma,t}(x)
   26:  :=g_t(x)\left[1+t^{-2}b_\Sigma
   27:  \left(\frac{x-\bar\gamma}{t}\right)\right]
   28: \end{equation}
   29: is a probability density.  Define
   30: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-defect-functional}
   31:  \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)
   32:  :=t^4D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t}).
   33: \end{equation}
   34: Then
   35: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition}
   36:  t^4H_d(t)
   37:  =\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)+\mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)+o(1)
   38:  \qquad(t\to\infty).
   39: \end{equation}
   40: Consequently,
   41: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-defect-equivalence}
   42:  t^4H_d(t)\longrightarrow\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
   43:  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
   44:  \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)\longrightarrow0.
   45: \end{equation}
   46: No density of \(\nu\), regular-variation hypothesis, angular balance, or
   47: higher absolute moment is required.
   48: \end{theorem}
   49: 
   50: \begin{proof}
   51: After translation, assume \(\bar\gamma=0\), and write
   52: \[
   53:  \Omega_d(\dd y)=P_1^{(d)}(y)\dd y,
   54:  \qquad
   55:  F_y(z)=\left(\frac{1+|y|^2}{1+|y-z|^2}\right)^{(d+1)/2}.
   56: \]
   57: In normalized coordinates,
   58: \[
   59:  q_t(y):=\frac{h_t(ty)}{g_t(ty)}
   60:  =\EE F_y(X/t),
   61:  \qquad \delta_t=q_t-1,
   62:  \qquad u_t=t^{-2}b_\Sigma.
   63: \]
   64: The covariance calculation in
   65: Theorem~\ref{thm:multidim-covariance-algebra} gives
   66: \(\int b_\Sigma\dd\Omega_d=0\).  Since \(b_\Sigma\) is bounded,
   67: \(1+u_t\ge1/2\) for all sufficiently large \(t\), proving that
   68: \eqref{eq:covariance-poisson-proxy} is positive and has integral one.
   69: 
   70: We first establish the only asymptotic input needed below:
   71: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-weak-pairing}
   72:  t^2\int_{\RR^d}b_\Sigma(y)\delta_t(y)\,\Omega_d(\dd y)
   73:  \longrightarrow
   74:  \int_{\RR^d}b_\Sigma(y)^2\,\Omega_d(\dd y).
   75: \end{equation}
   76: Set
   77: \[
   78:  J(z)=\int_{\RR^d}b_\Sigma(y)F_y(z)\,\Omega_d(\dd y)
   79:  =\int_{\RR^d}b_\Sigma(y)P_1^{(d)}(y-z)\dd y.
   80: \]
   81: The function \(b_\Sigma\) is even, smooth, and has bounded second
   82: derivatives.  Translation in the last integral shows that \(J\) is even,
   83: \(J(0)=0\), and its Hessian is bounded on the unit ball.  Hence
   84: \(|J(z)|\le C|z|^2\) for \(|z|\le1\).  For \(|z|\ge1\), positivity and
   85: normalization of the translated Poisson density give
   86: \(|J(z)|\le\|b_\Sigma\|_\infty\le
   87: \|b_\Sigma\|_\infty|z|^2\).  Thus
   88: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-global-quadratic-pairing}
   89:  |J(z)|\le C_{d,\Sigma}|z|^2,
   90:  \qquad z\in\RR^d.
   91: \end{equation}
   92: 
   93: Let \(B_2(y,z)\) denote the homogeneous degree-two Taylor term of
   94: \(F_y(z)\) at zero.  Taylor expansion under the integral gives
   95: \[
   96:  J(z)=\int b_\Sigma(y)B_2(y,z)\,\Omega_d(\dd y)+o(|z|^2).
   97: \]
   98: The covariance-mode identity proved in
   99: Theorem~\ref{thm:multidim-covariance-algebra} is
  100: \(\EE B_2(y,X)=b_\Sigma(y)\).  Since \(\EE|X|^2<\infty\),
  101: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-global-quadratic-pairing} and dominated
  102: convergence imply
  103: \[
  104:  \EE[t^2J(X/t)]
  105:  \longrightarrow
  106:  \int b_\Sigma(y)\EE B_2(y,X)\,\Omega_d(\dd y)
  107:  =\int b_\Sigma^2\dd\Omega_d.
  108: \]
  109: Because \(\int b_\Sigma\dd\Omega_d=0\), the left side is precisely the
  110: left side of \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-weak-pairing}.
  111: 
  112: We also have
  113: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-l1-smallness}
  114:  \|\delta_t\|_{L^1(\Omega_d)}\longrightarrow0.
  115: \end{equation}
  116: Indeed, Minkowski's integral inequality bounds this norm by
  117: \[
  118:  \EE\left\|P_1^{(d)}(\mathord\cdot-X/t)-P_1^{(d)}
  119:  \right\|_{L^1(\RR^d)},
  120: \]
  121: which tends to zero by translation continuity and dominated convergence.
  122: 
  123: Put \(r_t=\delta_t-u_t\).  Equations
  124: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-weak-pairing} and
  125: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-l1-smallness} give
  126: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-remainder-pairing}
  127:  t^2\int b_\Sigma r_t\dd\Omega_d\longrightarrow0,
  128:  \qquad \|r_t\|_{L^1(\Omega_d)}\longrightarrow0.
  129: \end{equation}
  130: Uniformly in \(y\),
  131: \[
  132:  \log(1+u_t)=u_t+O(t^{-4}).
  133: \]
  134: It follows from \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-remainder-pairing} that
  135: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-cross-remainder}
  136:  \int r_t\log(1+u_t)\dd\Omega_d=o(t^{-4}).
  137: \end{equation}
  138: On the other hand, with
  139: \(\Phi(s)=(1+s)\log(1+s)-s\), boundedness of \(b_\Sigma\) gives
  140: \begin{align}
  141:  \int(1+u_t)\log(1+u_t)\dd\Omega_d
  142:  &=\int\Phi(u_t)\dd\Omega_d \notag\\
  143:  &=\frac{t^{-4}}2\int b_\Sigma^2\dd\Omega_d+O(t^{-6})
  144:  =t^{-4}\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)+O(t^{-6}).
  145:  \label{eq:covariance-proxy-self-cross-entropy}
  146: \end{align}
  147: Combining \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-cross-remainder} and
  148: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-self-cross-entropy} yields
  149: \begin{equation}\label{eq:covariance-proxy-cross-entropy-asymptotic}
  150:  \int(1+\delta_t)\log(1+u_t)\dd\Omega_d
  151:  =t^{-4}\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)+o(t^{-4}).
  152: \end{equation}
  153: 
  154: Finally, the exact logarithmic identity is
  155: \[
  156:  H_d(t)=D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t})
  157:  +\int(1+\delta_t)\log(1+u_t)\dd\Omega_d.
  158: \]
  159:  All terms are finite for large \(t\).  Indeed, convexity of relative entropy
  160:  and
  161:  \[
  162:   D_{\rm KL}\!\left(P_1^{(d)}(\mathord\cdot-z)\,\middle\|\,
  163:   P_1^{(d)}\right)
  164:   \le \frac{d+1}{2}\log\{2(1+|z|^2)\}
  165:   \le C_d(1+|z|^2)
  166:  \]
  167:  bound \(H_d(t)\) by an integrable average under
  168:  \(\EE|X|^2<\infty\).  The first inequality follows from
  169:  \(1+|w+z|^2\le2(1+|w|^2)(1+|z|^2)\).  Moreover,
  170:  \(\log(1+u_t)\) is bounded.  Substitution of
  171: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-cross-entropy-asymptotic} proves
  172: \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition}.  Nonnegativity of relative
  173: entropy then gives \eqref{eq:covariance-proxy-defect-equivalence}.
  174: \end{proof}
  175: 
  176: \begin{corollary}[Proxy characterization at the critical vague boundary]
  177: \label{cor:critical-rv-proxy-characterization}
  178: Fix \(d\ge4\), set
  179: \[
  180:  p_d=\frac{4(d+1)}{d+5},
  181:  \qquad
  182:  \mu_\sigma(\dd\rho,\dd\theta)
  183:  =p_d\rho^{-p_d-1}\dd\rho\,\sigma(\dd\theta),
  184: \]
  185: and let \(\nu\) have mean \(\bar\gamma\), finite covariance \(\Sigma\), and
  186: centred law \(\nu_c\).  Suppose that, for a slowly varying \(L\),
  187: \[
  188:  \frac{r^{p_d}}{L(r)}\nu_c(r\,\mathord\cdot)
  189:  \xrightarrow[r\to\infty]{v}\mu_\sigma
  190:  \quad\text{on }\RR^d\setminus\{0\},
  191:  \qquad
  192:  \ell_L(t):=\int_{R_0}^t\frac{L(s)}s\dd s\longrightarrow\infty.
  193: \]
  194: Then
  195: \[
  196:  t^4H_d(t)\longrightarrow\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
  197:  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
  198:  \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)\longrightarrow0.
  199: \]
  200: In particular, for the two laws \(\nu_{\rm ac}\) and \(\nu_{\rm cl}\) in
  201: Theorem~\ref{thm:critical-vague-tail-nonidentifiability},
  202: \[
  203:  \mathcal C_{\nu_{\rm ac},\Sigma}(t)\longrightarrow0,
  204:  \qquad
  205:  \limsup_{t\to\infty}\mathcal C_{\nu_{\rm cl},\Sigma}(t)=+\infty.
  206: \]
  207: Thus the spectral measure and slowly varying factor do not determine the
  208: covariance-proxy defect trajectory.
  209: \end{corollary}
  210: 
  211: \begin{proof}
  212: The equivalence is Theorem~\ref{thm:covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition};
  213: the regular-variation assumptions specify the critical class but are not
  214: needed for that decomposition.  Its combination with
  215: \eqref{eq:critical-vague-tail-contrasting-entropy} gives the two final
  216: assertions.
  217: \end{proof}

你所提出的有限协方差 Poisson 代理缺陷分解，经独立重构后，其核心交叉熵估计可以成立；然而，关于“多个壳层在同一 Poisson 单元内聚合后不能通过壳层间抵消而规避检测”的表述不成立。设同一粗分割单元 $A=A_1\sqcup A_2$，代理质量分别为 $s_1,s_2$，而目标质量分别为 $p_1=s_1+\varepsilon$、$p_2=s_2-\varepsilon$。则 $p(A)=s(A)$，故该粗单元对应的 Bernoulli KL 恰为零，尽管在细分 $\{A_1,A_2\}$ 上的离散 KL 严格为正。请据此删除“同一单元内不可抵消”的断言，并给出严格的修正命题：只有沿生成 Borel $\sigma$-代数的分割序列充分细化时，局部差异才由完整相对熵恢复；任何固定粗分割均可能掩盖子单元之间的符号相反偏差。此外，有限分割变分表示与 Bernoulli 数据处理下界属于经典相对熵理论，不得作为本文的新定理重复提出。

本文当前最尖锐的开放问题是：在临界指数 $p_d=4(d+1)/(d+5)$ 的多元正则变动边界上，能否直接以原始尾测度的跨尺度局部几何性质，而非以定义上等价的 KL 缺陷轨道本身，给出 $\mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t)\to0$ 的严格充要条件？请提出一个可检验、对角向聚集与径向跨壳层聚集同时敏感、且严格强于模糊收敛而弱于有限 $p_d$ 阶绝对矩的条件；证明其充分性与必要性，明确处理原子律，并构造显示每项假设不可删除的反例。不得将有限分割变分公式、数据处理不等式或既有 $L^{q_d}$ 充分条件改写为答案。

不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+    p_density, q_density = sp.symbols("p_density q_density", positive=True)
+    phi_delta = p_density * sp.log(p_density) - p_density + 1

+def finite_covariance_proxy_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Stress the proxy cross-entropy asymptotic on anisotropic atomic laws."""
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    sample_power = 18
+    times = (4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+
+    for d in (4, 7):
+        sobol = qmc.Sobol(d=d + 1, scramble=True, seed=1729 + d)
+        uniform = sobol.random_base2(sample_power)
+        normal = ndtri(np.clip(uniform, 1.0e-14, 1.0 - 1.0e-14))
+        y = normal[:, :d] / np.abs(normal[:, d, None])
+        y_norm_sq = np.sum(y * y, axis=1)
+
+        pair_masses = np.linspace(0.45 / d, 0.75 / d, d)
+        pair_masses *= 0.72 / np.sum(pair_masses)
+        radii = np.linspace(0.65, 2.15, d)
+        covariance = np.diag(pair_masses * radii**2)
+        trace = float(np.trace(covariance))
+        covariance_zero = covariance - trace * np.eye(d) / d
+        iso = 3 * (d + 1) * (7 * d + 9) / (
+            4 * d * (d + 3) * (d + 5) * (d + 7)
+        )
+        traceless = 3 * (d + 1) * (d + 3) / (4 * (d + 5) * (d + 7))
+        q_value = iso * trace**2 + traceless * float(
+            np.sum(covariance_zero * covariance_zero)
+        )
+
+        quadratic = np.einsum("ni,ij,nj->n", y, covariance, y)
+        b_mode = (d + 1) / 2 * (
+            -trace / (1 + y_norm_sq)
+            + (d + 3) * quadratic / (1 + y_norm_sq) ** 2
+        )
+
+        cross_ratios = []
+        scaled_defects = []
+        for time in times:
+            quotient = np.full(y.shape[0], 1.0 - np.sum(pair_masses))
+            for axis, (mass, radius) in enumerate(zip(pair_masses, radii)):
+                shift = radius / time
+                dot = y[:, axis]
+                plus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 - 2 * shift * dot
+                minus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 + 2 * shift * dot
+                quotient += 0.5 * mass * (
+                    ((1 + y_norm_sq) / plus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                    + ((1 + y_norm_sq) / minus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                )
+
+            delta = quotient - 1
+            u = b_mode / time**2
+            remainder = delta - u
+            proxy_entropy = np.mean(
+                (1 + u) * np.log1p(u) - u
+                + remainder * np.log1p(u)
+            )
+            proxy_divergence = np.mean(
+                (1 + delta) * np.log((1 + delta) / (1 + u))
+                - remainder
+            )
+            cross_ratios.append(time**4 * proxy_entropy / q_value)
+            scaled_defects.append(time**4 * proxy_divergence)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            all(np.isfinite(cross_ratios))
+            and all(np.isfinite(scaled_defects))
+            and all(value >= -2.0e-10 for value in scaled_defects)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < (0.03 if quick else 0.012)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < abs(cross_ratios[0] - 1.0)
+            and scaled_defects[-1] < scaled_defects[0]
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}: t^4 cross/Q={cross_ratios}, "
+            f"t^4 D(h||k)={scaled_defects}"
+        )
+
+    return Check(
+        "finite-covariance proxy asymptotic stress",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+

+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+
 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

+def finite_covariance_proxy_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Stress the proxy cross-entropy asymptotic on anisotropic atomic laws."""
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    sample_power = 18
+    times = (4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+
+    for d in (4, 7):
+        sobol = qmc.Sobol(d=d + 1, scramble=True, seed=1729 + d)
+        uniform = sobol.random_base2(sample_power)
+        normal = ndtri(np.clip(uniform, 1.0e-14, 1.0 - 1.0e-14))
+        y = normal[:, :d] / np.abs(normal[:, d, None])
+        y_norm_sq = np.sum(y * y, axis=1)
+
+        pair_masses = np.linspace(0.45 / d, 0.75 / d, d)
+        pair_masses *= 0.72 / np.sum(pair_masses)
+        radii = np.linspace(0.65, 2.15, d)
+        covariance = np.diag(pair_masses * radii**2)
+        trace = float(np.trace(covariance))
+        covariance_zero = covariance - trace * np.eye(d) / d
+        iso = 3 * (d + 1) * (7 * d + 9) / (
+            4 * d * (d + 3) * (d + 5) * (d + 7)
+        )
+        traceless = 3 * (d + 1) * (d + 3) / (4 * (d + 5) * (d + 7))
+        q_value = iso * trace**2 + traceless * float(
+            np.sum(covariance_zero * covariance_zero)
+        )
+
+        quadratic = np.einsum("ni,ij,nj->n", y, covariance, y)
+        b_mode = (d + 1) / 2 * (
+            -trace / (1 + y_norm_sq)
+            + (d + 3) * quadratic / (1 + y_norm_sq) ** 2
+        )
+
+        cross_ratios = []
+        scaled_defects = []
+        for time in times:
+            quotient = np.full(y.shape[0], 1.0 - np.sum(pair_masses))
+            for axis, (mass, radius) in enumerate(zip(pair_masses, radii)):
+                shift = radius / time
+                dot = y[:, axis]
+                plus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 - 2 * shift * dot
+                minus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 + 2 * shift * dot
+                quotient += 0.5 * mass * (
+                    ((1 + y_norm_sq) / plus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                    + ((1 + y_norm_sq) / minus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                )
+
+            delta = quotient - 1
+            u = b_mode / time**2
+            remainder = delta - u
+            proxy_entropy = np.mean(
+                (1 + u) * np.log1p(u) - u
+                + remainder * np.log1p(u)
+            )
+            proxy_divergence = np.mean(
+                (1 + delta) * np.log((1 + delta) / (1 + u))
+                - remainder
+            )
+            cross_ratios.append(time**4 * proxy_entropy / q_value)
+            scaled_defects.append(time**4 * proxy_divergence)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            all(np.isfinite(cross_ratios))
+            and all(np.isfinite(scaled_defects))
+            and all(value >= -2.0e-10 for value in scaled_defects)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < (0.03 if quick else 0.012)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < abs(cross_ratios[0] - 1.0)
+            and scaled_defects[-1] < scaled_defects[0]
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}: t^4 cross/Q={cross_ratios}, "
+            f"t^4 D(h||k)={scaled_defects}"
+        )
+
+    return Check(
+        "finite-covariance proxy asymptotic stress",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+

+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+
 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

**(i) Per-Claim Verdicts**

- **ALREADY-IN-PAPER: covariance mode \(b_\Sigma\), its zero-mass identity, and \(\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)=\frac12\int b_\Sigma^2\,d\Omega_d\).** These are already established in `thm:multidim-covariance-algebra`; finite-covariance pointwise second-order convergence and the Fatou lower bound are already in `lem:finite-covariance-fatou-lower-bound`. See [sec_entropy_core_main.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/sec_entropy_core_main.tex:2243) and [sec_verified_A2_results.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/sec_verified_A2_results.tex:154).

- **ALREADY-PUBLISHED: the finite-partition representation**
  \[
  D(\mu\|\kappa)=\sup_N\sum_{A\in\Pi_N}\mu(A)\log\frac{\mu(A)}{\kappa(A)}.
  \]
  For nested finite partitions generating the Borel \(\sigma\)-algebra, this follows from conditional-expectation convergence applied to \(d\mu/d\kappa\) and convexity of \(x\log x\). It is part of the classical Csiszár \(f\)-divergence/coarse-graining theory, not a new Poisson theorem: I. Csiszár, *Information-type measures of difference of probability distributions and indirect observations*, Studia Sci. Math. Hungar. 2 (1967), 299–318; see [main.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/main.tex:352).

- **CORRECT-AND-NEW: the finite-covariance proxy cross-entropy asymptotic and defect decomposition**
  \[
  t^4H_d(t)=\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
  +t^4D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t})+o(1).
  \]
  Independently reconstructed proof: \(b_\Sigma\) is bounded and has zero \(\Omega_d\)-mean, so \(k_{\Sigma,t}\) is a probability density for sufficiently large \(t\). Writing
  \[
  J(z)=\int b_\Sigma(y)P_1^{(d)}(y-z)\,dy,
  \]
  evenness gives \(J(0)=DJ(0)=0\); bounded second derivatives give \(J(z)=O(|z|^2)\) locally, while boundedness of \(b_\Sigma\) gives the same global bound. Hence finite covariance permits dominated convergence and proves
  \[
  t^2\int b_\Sigma\delta_t\,d\Omega_d\to\int b_\Sigma^2\,d\Omega_d.
  \]
  Translation continuity gives \(\|\delta_t\|_1\to0\). With \(r_t=\delta_t-t^{-2}b_\Sigma\), these facts imply
  \[
  \int r_t\log(1+t^{-2}b_\Sigma)\,d\Omega_d=o(t^{-4}),
  \]
  while the bounded Taylor expansion of the proxy contributes
  \(t^{-4}\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)+O(t^{-6})\). The exact logarithmic chain identity then proves the decomposition. No density or regular-variation hypothesis is used.

- **CORRECT-AND-NEW: the necessary-and-sufficient condition**
  \[
  t^4H_d(t)\to\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
  \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t):=
  t^4D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t})\to0.
  \]
  This follows in both directions from the preceding decomposition and nonnegativity of relative entropy. It holds for every finite-covariance law and therefore also throughout the stated critical regularly varying class. The pre-existing manuscript contained only Fatou lower bounds and sufficient \(L^2/L^{q_d}\) criteria, not this proxy orbit or decomposition.

- **ALREADY-PUBLISHED: the Bernoulli lower bound and detection by angular cells.** The inequality
  \[
  D(\mu\|\kappa)\ge
  D_{\rm Ber}(\mu(A)\|\kappa(A))
  \]
  is the classical data-processing inequality. It correctly detects an aggregate discrepancy on a selected cap or annulus, but is not new.

- **WRONG: “cross-shell aggregation cannot be avoided by cancellation.”** Let \(A=A_1\sqcup A_2\), with proxy masses \(s_1,s_2\) and target masses
  \(p_1=s_1+\varepsilon\), \(p_2=s_2-\varepsilon\). Then \(p(A)=s(A)\), so the Bernoulli KL on the coarse cell \(A\) is exactly zero, although the refined partition \(\{A_1,A_2\}\) has strictly positive discrete KL. Only refinement to a generating \(\sigma\)-algebra recovers the complete divergence; no fixed coarse Poisson cell prevents cancellation.

- The novelty search found no competing theorem: six targeted arXiv API queries returned zero exact matches; broader searches returned four unrelated “Poisson kernel + entropy” records and sixteen unrelated “Cauchy + relative entropy” records. Five targeted Crossref searches likewise found no harmonic-Poisson covariance-proxy decomposition. The audit is recorded in [literature_check.md](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/artifacts/literature_check.md:118).

**(ii) Integration and Refusals**

Integrated `thm:covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition`, with alias `thm:finite-covariance-proxy-defect-decomposition`, and the full critical-class `cor:critical-rv-proxy-characterization` in [sec_covariance_proxy_defect.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/sec_covariance_proxy_defect.tex:11). The abstract and introduction now state the result as *asymptotically exact*, avoiding an “exact” overclaim for a formula containing \(o(1)\).

The finite-partition theorem and Bernoulli data processing were refused as new manuscript results because they are classical. The shellwise noncancellation assertion was refused because of the explicit two-subcell counterexample above. No git commit was created.

**(iii) Verification Script**

`python artifacts/verify_oracle_A2.py` completed with exit code `0`, `failures=[]`, and `RESULT: PASS`. New checks cover proxy normalization, exact KL chain algebra, positivity thresholds, radial quadrature in \(d=4,7,11\), and anisotropic atomic laws in \(d=4,7\). +def finite_covariance_proxy_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Stress the proxy cross-entropy asymptotic on anisotropic atomic laws."""
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    sample_power = 18
+    times = (4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+
+    for d in (4, 7):
+        sobol = qmc.Sobol(d=d + 1, scramble=True, seed=1729 + d)
+        uniform = sobol.random_base2(sample_power)
+        normal = ndtri(np.clip(uniform, 1.0e-14, 1.0 - 1.0e-14))
+        y = normal[:, :d] / np.abs(normal[:, d, None])
+        y_norm_sq = np.sum(y * y, axis=1)
+
+        pair_masses = np.linspace(0.45 / d, 0.75 / d, d)
+        pair_masses *= 0.72 / np.sum(pair_masses)
+        radii = np.linspace(0.65, 2.15, d)
+        covariance = np.diag(pair_masses * radii**2)
+        trace = float(np.trace(covariance))
+        covariance_zero = covariance - trace * np.eye(d) / d
+        iso = 3 * (d + 1) * (7 * d + 9) / (
+            4 * d * (d + 3) * (d + 5) * (d + 7)
+        )
+        traceless = 3 * (d + 1) * (d + 3) / (4 * (d + 5) * (d + 7))
+        q_value = iso * trace**2 + traceless * float(
+            np.sum(covariance_zero * covariance_zero)
+        )
+
+        quadratic = np.einsum("ni,ij,nj->n", y, covariance, y)
+        b_mode = (d + 1) / 2 * (
+            -trace / (1 + y_norm_sq)
+            + (d + 3) * quadratic / (1 + y_norm_sq) ** 2
+        )
+
+        cross_ratios = []
+        scaled_defects = []
+        for time in times:
+            quotient = np.full(y.shape[0], 1.0 - np.sum(pair_masses))
+            for axis, (mass, radius) in enumerate(zip(pair_masses, radii)):
+                shift = radius / time
+                dot = y[:, axis]
+                plus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 - 2 * shift * dot
+                minus_denominator = 1 + y_norm_sq + shift**2 + 2 * shift * dot
+                quotient += 0.5 * mass * (
+                    ((1 + y_norm_sq) / plus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                    + ((1 + y_norm_sq) / minus_denominator) ** ((d + 1) / 2)
+                )
+
+            delta = quotient - 1
+            u = b_mode / time**2
+            remainder = delta - u
+            proxy_entropy = np.mean(
+                (1 + u) * np.log1p(u) - u
+                + remainder * np.log1p(u)
+            )
+            proxy_divergence = np.mean(
+                (1 + delta) * np.log((1 + delta) / (1 + u))
+                - remainder
+            )
+            cross_ratios.append(time**4 * proxy_entropy / q_value)
+            scaled_defects.append(time**4 * proxy_divergence)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            all(np.isfinite(cross_ratios))
+            and all(np.isfinite(scaled_defects))
+            and all(value >= -2.0e-10 for value in scaled_defects)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < (0.03 if quick else 0.012)
+            and abs(cross_ratios[-1] - 1.0) < abs(cross_ratios[0] - 1.0)
+            and scaled_defects[-1] < scaled_defects[0]
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}: t^4 cross/Q={cross_ratios}, "
+            f"t^4 D(h||k)={scaled_defects}"
+        )
+
+    return Check(
+        "finite-covariance proxy asymptotic stress",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+
+

+def covariance_proxy_quadrature_check(quick: bool) -> Check:
+    """Test the proxy chain identity and cross-entropy coefficient in d>=4."""
+
+    node_count = 70 if quick else 130
+    nodes, weights = leggauss(node_count)
+    theta = (math.pi / 4) * (nodes + 1)
+    theta_weights = (math.pi / 4) * weights
+    phi = (math.pi / 2) * (nodes + 1)
+    phi_weights = (math.pi / 2) * weights
+    radius_nodes = np.tan(theta)
+
+    rows = []
+    passed = True
+    times = (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0) if quick else (2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0, 20.0)
+    for d, sphere_radius in ((4, 0.8), (7, 1.3), (11, 1.7)):
+        radial_weights = theta_weights * np.sin(theta) ** (d - 1)
+        radial_weights /= np.sum(radial_weights)
+        angular_weights = phi_weights * np.sin(phi) ** (d - 2)
+        angular_weights /= np.sum(angular_weights)
+        cosines = np.cos(phi)
+        exponent = (d + 1) / 2
+        variance = sphere_radius**2 / d
+        radius_squared = radius_nodes**2
+        b_sigma = exponent * (
+            -sphere_radius**2 / (1 + radius_squared)
+            + (d + 3)
+            * variance
+            * radius_squared
+            / (1 + radius_squared) ** 2
+        )
+        q_sigma = 0.5 * float(np.sum(radial_weights * b_sigma**2))
+
+        ratios = []
+        identity_errors = []
+        minimum_defect = math.inf
+        rejected_times = []
+        valid_times = []
+        for time in times:
+            epsilon = sphere_radius / time
+            denominator = (
+                1
+                + radius_squared[:, None]
+                + epsilon**2
+                - 2 * radius_nodes[:, None] * epsilon * cosines[None, :]
+            )
+            quotient_values = np.sum(
+                angular_weights[None, :]
+                * ((1 + radius_squared[:, None]) / denominator) ** exponent,
+                axis=1,
+            )
+            proxy_values = 1 + b_sigma / time**2
+            if np.min(proxy_values) <= 0:
+                rejected_times.append(time)
+                continue
+            valid_times.append(time)
+            entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values)
+                )
+            )
+            defect = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(quotient_values / proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            cross_entropy = float(
+                np.sum(
+                    radial_weights
+                    * quotient_values
+                    * np.log(proxy_values)
+                )
+            )
+            identity_errors.append(abs(entropy - defect - cross_entropy))
+            minimum_defect = min(minimum_defect, defect)
+            ratios.append(time**4 * cross_entropy / q_sigma)
+
+        row_ok = (
+            max(identity_errors) < 2.0e-13
+            and minimum_defect > -2.0e-13
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < (0.012 if quick else 0.005)
+            and abs(ratios[-1] - 1) < abs(ratios[0] - 1)
+        )
+        passed = passed and row_ok
+        rows.append(
+            f"d={d}, sphere radius={sphere_radius}: "
+            f"valid t={valid_times}, positivity-gate rejections={rejected_times}, "
+            f"t^4 cross/Q ratios={ratios}, "
+            f"max chain residual={max(identity_errors):.3e}, "
+            f"min proxy KL={minimum_defect:.3e}"
+        )
+    return Check(
+        "covariance proxy multidimensional quadrature",
+        passed,
+        "; ".join(rows),
+    )
+

 def quotient(y: mp.mpf, t: mp.mpf, law: Sequence[tuple[mp.mpf, mp.mpf]]) -> mp.mpf:
     return sum(weight * (1 + y * y) / (1 + (y - location / t) ** 2) for weight, location in law)

**(i) Per-Claim Verdicts**

- **ALREADY-IN-PAPER: covariance mode \(b_\Sigma\), its zero-mass identity, and \(\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)=\frac12\int b_\Sigma^2\,d\Omega_d\).** These are already established in `thm:multidim-covariance-algebra`; finite-covariance pointwise second-order convergence and the Fatou lower bound are already in `lem:finite-covariance-fatou-lower-bound`. See [sec_entropy_core_main.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/sec_entropy_core_main.tex:2243) and [sec_verified_A2_results.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/sec_verified_A2_results.tex:154).

- **ALREADY-PUBLISHED: the finite-partition representation**
  \[
  D(\mu\|\kappa)=\sup_N\sum_{A\in\Pi_N}\mu(A)\log\frac{\mu(A)}{\kappa(A)}.
  \]
  For nested finite partitions generating the Borel \(\sigma\)-algebra, this follows from conditional-expectation convergence applied to \(d\mu/d\kappa\) and convexity of \(x\log x\). It is part of the classical Csiszár \(f\)-divergence/coarse-graining theory, not a new Poisson theorem: I. Csiszár, *Information-type measures of difference of probability distributions and indirect observations*, Studia Sci. Math. Hungar. 2 (1967), 299–318; see [main.tex](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/main.tex:352).

- **CORRECT-AND-NEW: the finite-covariance proxy cross-entropy asymptotic and defect decomposition**
  \[
  t^4H_d(t)=\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
  +t^4D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t})+o(1).
  \]
  Independently reconstructed proof: \(b_\Sigma\) is bounded and has zero \(\Omega_d\)-mean, so \(k_{\Sigma,t}\) is a probability density for sufficiently large \(t\). Writing
  \[
  J(z)=\int b_\Sigma(y)P_1^{(d)}(y-z)\,dy,
  \]
  evenness gives \(J(0)=DJ(0)=0\); bounded second derivatives give \(J(z)=O(|z|^2)\) locally, while boundedness of \(b_\Sigma\) gives the same global bound. Hence finite covariance permits dominated convergence and proves
  \[
  t^2\int b_\Sigma\delta_t\,d\Omega_d\to\int b_\Sigma^2\,d\Omega_d.
  \]
  Translation continuity gives \(\|\delta_t\|_1\to0\). With \(r_t=\delta_t-t^{-2}b_\Sigma\), these facts imply
  \[
  \int r_t\log(1+t^{-2}b_\Sigma)\,d\Omega_d=o(t^{-4}),
  \]
  while the bounded Taylor expansion of the proxy contributes
  \(t^{-4}\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)+O(t^{-6})\). The exact logarithmic chain identity then proves the decomposition. No density or regular-variation hypothesis is used.

- **CORRECT-AND-NEW: the necessary-and-sufficient condition**
  \[
  t^4H_d(t)\to\mathcal Q_d(\Sigma)
  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
  \mathcal C_{\nu,\Sigma}(t):=
  t^4D_{\rm KL}(h_t\|k_{\Sigma,t})\to0.
  \]
  This follows in both directions from the preceding decomposition and nonnegativity of relative entropy. It holds for every finite-covariance law and therefore also throughout the stated critical regularly varying class. The pre-existing manuscript contained only Fatou lower bounds and sufficient \(L^2/L^{q_d}\) criteria, not this proxy orbit or decomposition.

- **ALREADY-PUBLISHED: the Bernoulli lower bound and detection by angular cells.** The inequality
  \[
  D(\mu\|\kappa)\ge
  D_{\rm Ber}(\mu(A)\|\kappa(A))
  \]
  is the classical data-processing inequality. It correctly detects an aggregate discrepancy on a selected cap or annulus, but is not new.

- **WRONG: “cross-shell aggregation cannot be avoided by cancellation.”** Let \(A=A_1\sqcup A_2\), with proxy masses \(s_1,s_2\) and target masses
  \(p_1=s_1+\varepsilon\), \(p_2=s_2-\varepsilon\). Then \(p(A)=s(A)\), so the Bernoulli KL on the coarse cell \(A\) is exactly zero, although the refined partition \(\{A_1,A_2\}\) has strictly positive discrete KL. Only refinement to a generating \(\sigma\)-algebra recovers the complete divergence; no fixed coarse Poisson cell prevents cancellation.

- The novelty search found no competing theorem: six targeted arXiv API queries returned zero exact matches; broader searches returned four unrelated “Poisson kernel + entropy” records and sixteen unrelated “Cauchy + relative entropy” records. Five targeted Crossref searches likewise found no harmonic-Poisson covariance-proxy decomposition. The audit is recorded in [literature_check.md](/D:/omega/automath/papers/publication/2026_cayley_chebyshev_poisson_entropy_strip_rkhs_jfa/artifacts/literature_check.md:118).

The abstract and introduction now state the result as *asymptotically exact*, avoiding an “exact” overclaim for a formula containing \(o(1)\).

The finite-partition theorem and Bernoulli data processing were refused as new manuscript results because they are classical. The shellwise noncancellation assertion was refused because of the explicit two-subcell counterexample above. No git commit was created.
